# 第 6 章　蕉恶非道•无忍义之战

> Banana Outrage: Battles Without Ninja and Humanity　·　蕉恶非道•无忍义之战

## 039　An Andalusian Monkey　一只安达鲁猴

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色：Trailblazer; Rappa; Welt; Himeko; Dan Heng; March 7th; Alley　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Stranger in a Strange Land'' completed

### View the message from the Crew

> *The usually quiet group chat for the Crew suddenly buzzes with the prompt of a new message. While it might not be a call to adventure in a new world, based on your previous experience, it must mark the start of a new journey. What could be the mysterious "news" Himeko mentioned? Head back to the Express and ask her about it!*

【游戏内短信 · Is anyone there?】

**Himeko:**

**Himeko:** Is anyone there?

> **姬子：**大家在吗？

**March 7th:** Yeah!

> **三月七：**在！

**Dan Heng:** Yes

> **丹恒：**在。

**Himeko:** I've got some news for you

**March 7th:** Whoa! Spill the beans!

**(Trailblazer):** Good news, I hope?

**Himeko:** Yeah, you could say that

**March 7th:** Great!

**(Trailblazer):** I have a feeling it might be bad news...

**March 7th:** Come on! Don't jinx it!

**Himeko:** Of course not. No worries

> **姬子：**有个消息想跟大家分享一下

- ▸ I've been crazy busy lately

**(Trailblazer):** I'm a little busy recently

**March 7th:** Yeah, he's/she's been on the move everywhere these days.

**March 7th:** ...You're not planning to leave us, are you?

> **三月七：**哇，什么消息？

**Himeko:** Let's catch up when you're free. Welt and I will be waiting for you in the Parlor Car

### Return to the Express and hear about the news from Himeko

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**March 7th:** (Trailblazer), you're finally here! We've been waiting for so long.

> **三月七：**开拓者，你可算来啦！都等你好半天了。

**Dan Heng:** It's only been five minutes.

> **丹恒：**也就五分钟而已。

**Himeko:** Since everyone's here, let's get down to business.

> **姬子：**就在刚才，星穹列车收到了来自「盛会之星」的邀请函……

**March 7th:** Hang on! Why am I getting a sense of deja vu?

> **三月七：**等等！怎么感觉这个桥段似曾相识？！

**March 7th:** Even the Planet of Festivities can't possibly hold a festival every day!

> **三月七：**就算是「盛会之星」也不能天天办盛会吧！

- ▸ Are we going to look for the Watchmaker's Legacy again?

**March 7th:** I don't think he had that many legacies to leave behind...

> **三月七：**那位前辈也没留下这么多遗产吧……

- ▸ Are we all still in a dream?

**March 7th:** Please don't! We don't need to go through that again!

> **三月七：**别啊，同一种套路别来第二遍了！

**Welt:** Don't worry — the Express received a normal invitation this time. It's from Penacony's "Paperfold University".

> **瓦尔特：**别紧张，这次列车收到的是一场普通邀约，来自匹诺康尼的学府「折纸大学」。

**Himeko:** The university plans to hold an academic conference on the topic of "Trailblazing" in the Moment of Sol. The Astral Express has been invited as guests to the conference.

> **姬子：**校方打算在「太阳的时刻」筹办一场有关「开拓」的学术研讨会，邀请星穹列车作为嘉宾出席。

**March 7th:** An academic conference... You mean we'll have to sit in an auditorium and spend several system hours talking about a few hundred slides' worth of scholarly discourse?

> **三月七：**学术研讨…是要坐在大讲堂里，对着几百页幻灯片聊上好几个系统时？

**Himeko:** Don't worry, you can leave such "boring" events to the grown-ups. I will attend the conference with Welt. As for the three of you...

> **姬子：**放心，这种「枯燥」的活动就交给大人来处理吧。我和瓦尔特会应邀参与研讨，而你们三人……

**Himeko:** If you are interested, you can enter the university as guest students.

> **姬子：**如果有兴趣，大家可以以「旁听生」的身份进入大学。

- ▸ Guest students?

**Welt:** Well, it happens to be the start of the academic term, and Paperfold University is preparing for its Dreamlight Anniversary. These annual festivities serve as both a welcoming gift for new students, and a chance to celebrate for those already enrolled.

> **瓦尔特：**时逢开学季，折纸大学正在筹办「彩梦校庆」，既是献给新生的开学礼物，也是在校生一年一度的狂欢庆典。

**Welt:** Why don't you youngsters join the new students? You could explore the campus, experience student life...

> **瓦尔特：**都是年轻人，你们不妨和新生一同入学，在校园里随便走走，体验下学生生活。

**March 7th:** D—Do we have to study?

> **三月七：**要、要读书吗……

**March 7th:** ...That's great! A young cute girl full of energy living her best life on campus? That experience is practically made for me!

> **三月七：**…真是太棒了！青春活泼的美少女在校园里挥洒人生，这种情节简直是为本姑娘量身定制的嘛！

**Dan Heng:** I hear that Paperfold University is well-known throughout the universe as a top arts school. I would love to check it out too.

> **丹恒：**据说「折纸大学」是寰宇皆知的顶尖艺术学府，我也很想见识一下。

- ▸ Campus life, here I come!

**Welt:** Campus life...

- ▸ Awesome! I'll finally have some academic credentials!

**March 7th:** That's a really practical way of looking at it...

> **三月七：**呃，你的关注点好现实哦……

> **瓦尔特：**恐怕要让你失望了，折纸大学不会为旁听生颁发凭证。但我相信，你会收获远比证书更有价值的事物。

- ▸ Help! I don't want to go to school!

> **瓦尔特：**不用担心，旁听生不会有繁重的学业。师生们也都在忙着筹备庆典，就当是参加一场派对吧。

**March 7th:** Campus life without homework or examinations? That sounds like paradise!

**Himeko:** Looks like everyone can't wait. Let's go ahead with that plan, then. I will submit the admission documents to Paperfold University. We'll leave once everyone is ready. Let's all have some fun.

> **姬子：**看来大家都已经等不及了，那就这么定了。我会向折纸大学递交入学材料，准备好了就出发，一定要玩得开心啊。

- ▸ That Galaxy Ranger?

### Head to your suite

> *Once more, you find yourself at The Reverie, the gateway into the Dreamscape that is imbued with many of your memories. There is no need to reflect on your past before diving into the Dreamscape, because each sleep births a brand-new sweet dream... or a nightmare. Get ready to dive into the Dreamscape. The hotel has assigned you the same room as last time, whether out of consideration for your nostalgia or sheer laziness to tidy up other rooms.*

*(Immediately after the previous dialogue)*

**(Trailblazer):** (I'm staying in the same room as the last time I was here... Time to enter the dream.)

> **开拓者：**（我的房间还是上次那间…准备入梦吧。）

*(Enter the suite)*

**Rash Bloodhound:** Hurry up! I hear that the major shareholders have already arrived at the lobby. Don't alarm them!

> **冒失的猎犬：**搞快点！听人说大股东们已经到大堂了，千万不能惊动了人家！

**Panicking Bloodhound:** I know! But that fella was running in this direction. How did he disappear so quickly...?

> **慌张的猎犬：**我知道啊！但那家伙明明就是往这个方向跑的，怎么一下子就没影了……

**Rash Bloodhound:** Shh! Someone else is here. Who are you?

> **冒失的猎犬：**嘘，有人闯进来了——你是什么人？

- ▸ I'm one of the Nameless on the Astral Express.

**Rash Bloodhound:** The what Express now? Don't try to sell me anything. My miserable pay can't afford to buy an Express of any sorts!

> **冒失的猎犬：**星什么车？别给我推销，我就这点儿工资，买不起车！

- ▸ I'm the Superstar from the Festivity Auditions.

**Rash Bloodhound:** The what Auditions? Sorry, I usually just relax in my free time and never watch variety shows!

> **冒失的猎犬：**盛什么选？抱歉，哥们我闲时都只顾沉淀，从来不看娱乐综艺！

- ▸ I'm a VIP.

**Rash Bloodhound:** Oh, cut it out. Everyone who comes here calls themselves a VIP. It doesn't mean anything to me by this point.

> **冒失的猎犬：**得了吧，哥们/姐们，来这的都说自己是大人物，我都听麻了。

**Rash Bloodhound:** I don't care who you are. The Bloodhound Family is investigating a case here. Please make way.

> **冒失的猎犬：**不管你是谁，猎犬家系正在这查案呢，麻烦绕个道……

**Panicking Bloodhound:** Wait a second! Gray hair, yellow eyes, ribbons that serve no purpose on his/her clothes... I think he/she's the major shareholder you were talking about!

> **慌张的猎犬：**喂！灰头发，黄眼睛，衣服上挂着几条莫名其妙的带子…他/她好像就是那位大股东！

**Rash Bloodhound:** What!? For the love of... Couldn't you have said that earlier!?

> **冒失的猎犬：**什么？！我的神主，你早说啊！

**Rash Bloodhound:** Ahem, I beg your pardon, esteemed guest. I was a bit brusque earlier. The Bloodhounds are just carrying out a routine inspection. All guest rooms and facilities are in good order, so please be at ease and enjoy your stay.

> **冒失的猎犬：**——哎呀，贵宾，我刚说话有点大声了，您多见谅！猎犬们只是在进行例行检查，现在客房设施一切正常，您尽管放心入住。

**Panicking Bloodhound:** But... the suspect...

> **慌张的猎犬：**可是犯人……

- ▸ Suspect?

**Rash Bloodhound:** You...! Sus... sustainable! Ah, yes, sustainable! We know frequent inspections may not seem sustainable to some, but these procedures ensure a safe and secure environment for guests, so the Bloodhounds are more than happy to do so. We'll take our leave for now. Please have a good rest!

> **冒失的猎犬：**你——啊对对对，烦人，确实烦人！但这些流程都是为了宾客们的安全，猎犬从不嫌麻烦！我们这就离开，您好好休息啊。

**Panicking Bloodhound:** Um... Please stay safe, and be careful in the dream...

> **慌张的猎犬：**那个…您一定要在梦中注意安全，务必小心……

**Rash Bloodhound:** Alright, that's enough. Come on, let's go!

> **冒失的猎犬：**差不多得了，赶紧跟我走！

### (Optional) Examine the items inside the room

*(Immediately after the previous dialogue)*

**(Trailblazer):** (I'm confused... Maybe I should look around before I enter the dream?)

> **开拓者：**（这都什么跟什么…要不在入梦前再检查一下？）

*(Investigate the chest)*

...

This treasure appears to be different from the one you previously encountered. Faced with your passion, it is unwilling to open up its heart, but choosing to fetter itself further.

- ▸ Open it with violence

You yell out something about trailblazing justice and courage, channeling all your strength into your arms, and attempt to make it open a crevice representing hope and fortune for you...

But it is useless! Its lid does not move an inch, like it was designed to never be opened. This reminds you of those unopenable doors along Penacony's streets.

- ▸ Watch closely

You scrutinize the chest in front of you, but don't notice any encrypted interfaces, hidden text, or item submission slots — it looks like it definitely can't be opened.

- ▸ Roll DEX once

You try using your dexterity to pry it open... but this isn't the Simulated Universe. There are no dice. You also don't have any lockpicking tools available, nor are your hands reliably nimble — the roll ends in Critical Failure.

*(Investigate the Strange Monkey Doll)*

**Strange Monkey Doll:** ...

> **奇怪的猴子玩偶：**……

You notice a strange... monkey doll in the room?

Judging from the materials, you can see the doll's craftsmanship is exquisite, but its visual design begs to differ. This sly look and eyes brimming with wit... Do people really like characters like this?

- ▸ Ignore it

You decide to ignore this monkey like you would ignore an insignificant pebble, a stray wind-blown leaf, or a roadside trashcan...

Hang on. You've never ignored a trashcan.

- ▸ Talk to it

You try to communicate with it, an act that would be slightly silly in other worlds, yet so reasonably normal on Penacony — which fan of Clockie hasn't had a heart-to-heart conversation with their own Clockie doll?

Pity that dolls can't talk and can only listen. For someone feeling lonely, however, listening is already enough.

- ▸ Punch it

You administer a swift punch to the monkey's face, its soft texture enveloping your fist. The monkey's visage accepts your anger — you definitely hit cotton.

You retract your iron fist imbued with the Trailblaze power, but its expression remains unchanged. You have struck it with violence, but it responds with a smile.

It silently sits in the middle of the room, mute and stoic. Looking at its dumb face, a voice inexplicably reaches your ears... "bana"?

*(Investigate the Strange Monkey Doll again, optional)*

It silently sits in the middle of the room, mute and stoic. Looking at its dumb face, a voice inexplicably reaches your ears... "bana"?

*(Investigate the table)*

There are several bizarre monkeys drawn on the film reel's cover on the table. The title reads: Remastered — Top 10 Slumbernana Monkey Classic Shots. It even has a limited edition serial number labeled on it in embossed gold lettering.

This appears to be a present prepared for you by The Reverie, a most considerate gesture — it would've been even more thoughtful if there were a projector in the room. There's nothing here to play the reel.

*(Investigate the table again, optional)*

Projectors that can play such reels have long been phased out in the universe, but you often see these reels appearing on the market selling for a pretty penny. They mostly end up as collectibles, showcased in their masters' cabinets to demonstrate the owner's artistic taste.

*(Investigate the couch)*

## 040　The 400 Bananas　四百蕉

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Penacony Paperfold University College　|　角色：Trailblazer; Rappa; Dan Heng; March 7th; "Assistant Director"; Mr. Reca; "Master Kucha"; Montana; Chaletka; Richie; Gabbana; "Profnana"　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''An Andalusian Monkey'' completed

### Reunite with Dan Heng and March 7th

> *You finally step into the real Paperfold University. The bizarre dream back then might just be a manifestation of your fear of school... But what is there to fear now that you've seen this glittering and magnificent campus, together with the passionate elite classmates? Let the rosy vitality of youth consume your gray! Go say hello to Dan Heng and March 7th, your future classmates.*

Previously on Cosmic Ninjutsu Inscriptions

A long, long time ago in a world far away, there was a city that flourished under the rule of a Ninja Hero for many generations. It was known as the Ninja Capital.

Under the protection of the Ninja Hero, the people of the city thrived. They lived and worked in peace and happiness could be felt even in the sewers of the Ninja Capital.

Until an evildoer known as Evil Ninja Osaru murdered the Ninja Hero and laid claim to the city. The happiness of days past disappeared in an instant.

56th Year of Ninja Era, the Ninja Capital

**"Master Kucha":** O child new to the way of the ninja, I recognize your determination. Henceforth, you shall be the successor to my Dazzling Ninjutsu.

### Attend Mr. Reca's audition

> *The examiner, known as Mr. Reca, has transformed the Tuning Test into his audition session. This may heighten anxiety for many, given the inherent stress associated with interviews. But such a scenario won't deter you, our Festive Superstar, right? The director may appear a bit eccentric, but in a universe where 80% of movies are AI-generated, maybe only geniuses with eccentricities can manage to stay in the industry.*

> **芮克先生：**识人是导演的基本功，演员的一言一行都在体现他的个性——比如你刚刚的台词表现出了「好奇」，而好奇是求知的动力。

**Mr. Reca:** Now, for the first question: Please, introduce yourself.

## 042　A Clockwork Banana　发条香蕉

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Penacony Paperfold University College　|　角色：Trailblazer; March 7th; Dan Heng; Rappa; Shatana; Montana; Richie; Mr. Reca; Gabbana; De Lux; "Master Kucha"; Dreamweaving BananAdvisor; Charmony BananAdvisor; Fortune BananAdvisor; Chaletka; "Profnana"　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''Reservoir Monkeys'' completed

### Attend the Dreamweaving class with your classmates

> *Leisurely break time is almost over, and it is crucial to be on time for your first lesson. Please do not be late, refrain from chatting in class, and focus on the lecture... although you might be tempted not to. Rules are made to be broken, after all. Head to your first class and hope that the Dreamweaver Academy is not actually getting students to start with manual labor.*

Previously on Cosmic Ninjutsu Inscriptions

To defeat Evil Ninja Osaru, this girl has become the successor to the Dazzling Ninjutsu, adopting the codename of Rappa, she began training under Master Kucha.

To help Rappa master the secrets of the ninja arts, Master Kucha subjected her to harsh trials. And Rappa, in her quest to liberate the Ninja Capital from darkness, determinedly endured every test.

Master Kucha is aware that Rappa is maturing into a true ninja, and her destined mission will soon unfold...

61st Year of Ninja Era, Dazzling Dojo, the Ninja Capital

...

**"Master Kucha":** Ninja Initiate Rappa.

> **「苦茶大师」：**忍徒•乱破。

**Rappa:** Yes, Master.

> **乱破：**弟子在。

**"Master Kucha":** You've trained under me for five years. After rigorous tests and trials, you've mastered hundreds of ninja techniques.

> **「苦茶大师」：**你拜入师门已有五年，不惧苦修的熬煎，不畏试炼的艰险，百般忍法皆已精娴。

**"Master Kucha":** I hereby bestow upon you the title of Dazzling Ninja, entrusting you to be my weapon to defeat the evil ninja and guide the Ninja Capital into the future.

> **「苦茶大师」：**今日我赐你「缭乱•忍者」之名，你将成为我等讨伐邪忍的尖兵，引领忍之都砥砺前行。

**Rappa:** Thank you, Master Kucha. This is my greatest honor.

> **乱破：**拜谢苦茶大师，弟子不胜荣幸。

**"Master Kucha":** Take the scroll with you, for within its pages lie the secrets of Dazzling Ninjutsu. It shall cast a brilliance that makes fiendlings fear and destroy the evil ninja's intricate matrices.

> **「苦茶大师」：**把这卷轴拿去吧。此乃缭乱•忍法的奥义所在，它能释放邪祟畏惧的异彩，将邪忍的阵法尽数破坏。

*(Talk to Broken Monkey)*

This dispirited little monkey shows a sad expression, which seems unusual because the monkeys you have seen are always happy.

- ▸ Here comes the Clockwork!
- ▸ I need a moment to get ready.

*(If Broken Monkey's Emotion is set to Calm)*

**Broken Monkey:** Bana, bana.

> **颓废的小猴：**蕉，蕉蕉。

Under the spell of Clockwork, the little monkey calms down.

The moment you shut its dial, you seemed to resonate with its calmness. You saw the conveyor belt's boxes, the hesitant crowds in shadowy streets, and... TVs?

Before you could process these emotions, the little monkey resolutely dashed off into the distance...

*(If Broken Monkey's Emotion is set to Happy)*

> **颓废的小猴：**蕉！蕉蕉！

Before you could process these emotions, the little monkey happily dashed off into the distance...

**Mr. Reca:** Give it your best shot, fledgling actor. I see great potential in you.

> **芮克先生：**加油啊，小演员，我很看好你。

**Nanabloom-chan:** I—I will live up to your expectations!

> **芭蕉花酱：**我一定不会让您失望的！

*(Approach "Profnana" in the dean's office)*

**"Profnana":** What a surprise, my dear students. Hmm, no appointment or a polite knock on the door... but, I welcome you banantheless.

> **「蕉授」：**亲爱的同学们，虽然你们既没有预约也没有敲门，但还是欢迎你们蕉。

**Rappa:** Cease the shady evasion! I urge you to come clean now! You're Evil Ninja Osaru's Fiendling Chief, aren't you!?

> **乱破：**不要再胡枝扯叶了，速速招来——阁下就是御猿•邪忍的邪祟•总长吧！

**"Profnana":** ...

> **「蕉授」：**……

**"Profnana":** Excuse me dear student, could you help me translate that?

> **「蕉授」：**旁边的同学，能帮我翻译一下吗？

**Dan Heng:** The short story is that a memetic virus has broken loose in Paperfold University. It's going around infecting the students' minds as we speak.

> **丹恒：**简而言之，折纸大学出现了一种模因病毒，正在大肆传播并感染学生们的心智。

**Dan Heng:** Slumbernana Monkey is that virus, Profnana. And its transmission was orchestrated by none other than your BananAdvisors.

> **丹恒：**蕉授，它就是「睡蕉小猴」，而散布这种病毒的正是你手下的蕉师。

**"Profnana":** ...

> **「蕉授」：**……

**"Profnana":** I had thought this class would last a little longer than this.

> **「蕉授」：**我还以为这堂课能拖得再久一些。

**Dan Heng:** Your confession came sooner than we expected. So, is this one of Dr. Primitive's schemes?

> **丹恒：**你的坦白比预想中更快。所以，这果然是原始博士的阴谋？

**"Profnana":** A scheme? Bana tsk, you're thinking too highly of this place! Penacony is nothing but a cesspool of riches. It will never be worthy of the great Doctor's sublime experiments on humans!

> **「蕉授」：**阴谋？蕉蕉蕉，未免太高看匹诺康尼了。这座只剩下铜臭味的金钱乐园，怎么配得上博士崇高的人类实验？

**"Profnana":** Here, it's just me. A second class research ape working in Penacony on my graduation thesis: An atavistic experiment centered around memetic entities.

> **「蕉授」：**这里只有我，一位「二等研究猿」，来匹诺康尼完成自己的毕业课题：基于「模因」的返祖实验。

**"Profnana":** My research is going along smoothly. People here adore Slumbernana Monkey, especially students from the youth demographic group. Soon, they'll create a new Synesthesia Dreamscape, "the Moment of Slumbernana," and foster its growth, expansion, and evolution...

> **「蕉授」：**课题很顺利，这里的人都爱睡蕉小猴，尤其是以学生为代表的年轻人。他们将创造出新的联觉梦境「睡蕉的时刻」，并催化它成长、扩张、甚至进化……

**Rappa:** The endless atrocities that Osaru unleashed onto the world... just to build an Evil Realm where a hundred malevolent spirits prowl at night?

> **乱破：**御猿屋戕害天下苍生，只为建立一座百祟•夜行的邪恶国度？

**"Profnana":** ...

> **「蕉授」：**……

**"Profnana":** Girl, I cannot begin to fathom your cryptic speech. It doesn't sound like you're speaking ill of me... Or are you?

> **「蕉授」：**这位同学，你的话真的很难懂，不会是在骂我吧？

**"Profnana":** ...Never mind. Ordinary folks will never understand the great cause of our atavistic experiment. How about a pop quiz before I clarify your doubts? Who knows how bananas reproduce?

> **「蕉授」：**也罢，庸人难以理解返祖实验的伟大。在传道解惑前，先来个随堂测验吧：同学们知道香蕉是如何繁殖的吗？

- ▸ Through seeds?
- ▸ Through pollination?
- ▸ Through Mokuton: Banana-jutsu!

**Rappa:** A terrible ninjutsu.

> **乱破：**真是糟糕的忍术。

**Dan Heng:** To maintain their taste, the majority of commercial bananas are produced through "asexual reproduction." Lacking seeds and pollen, they are grown from the same rhizome.

> **丹恒：**为了保证口感，大多数商品香蕉都是「无性繁殖」。没有种子也没有花粉，需要用相同的根茎培育芽苗。

**"Profnana":** That's correct. Five Amber Eras ago, the Intelligentsia Guild cultivated a banana species that humans deemed perfect: the golden Slumbernana.

> **「蕉授」：**回答正确。五个琥珀纪前，博识学会培育出了一种人类自认完美的香蕉，那便是「金黄睡蕉」。

**"Profnana":** The golden Slumbernana swiftly dominated the fruit market. Nutritious, sweet, and its peel gives off a mesmerizing golden sheen. It transformed every interstellar banana plantation into a spectacle akin to the Golden Hour at night.

> **「蕉授」：**金黄睡蕉迅速统治了水果市场，它营养丰富、味道甜美、表皮还会散发出迷人的金色光泽，让每个星际香蕉园的夜晚都如同「黄金的时刻」一般闪耀。

**Dan Heng:** But it eventually became extinct, didn't it?

> **丹恒：**但它最后灭绝了，对吗？

**"Profnana":** Exactly. Because it was cultivated by humans, the Slumbernana lacked room for evolution. Having the same set of genes also meant sharing the same vulnerabilities. And when a fatal cosmic fungus wormed its way into the production line...

> **「蕉授」：**没错，只能人工繁殖的睡蕉没有进化的可能性，相同的基因赋予了它们相同的弱点。当一种致命的太空真菌随着生产线开始传播……

**"Profnana":** The same banana species that reigned over all the others of its kind was scrubbed clean from the entire cosmos in just mere months.

> **「蕉授」：**这一统治寰宇的物种在短短几个月内便消亡了。

**Dan Heng:** Are you saying that humans will suffer the same fate?

> **丹恒：**你想说，人类也是一样？

**"Profnana":** Of course. The golden Slumbernana's predecessor was the highly reproductive jadegreen banana. If not for human intervention, it would have blossomed much more vibrantly over the years, much like Penacony as we know it.

> **「蕉授」：**是啊，睡蕉的前身是繁殖力极强的碧绿野蕉。如果没有人为干预，它本应绽放更加多彩的光芒，就像这匹诺康尼一样。

**"Profnana":** It's a pity those on Penacony just had to be second-rate copycats. What with constructing skyscrapers, building cities, printing currencies, and even making the decision to become like the other cogs in the cosmic marketplace.

> **「蕉授」：**但这里的人们却选择了粗劣的模仿：修筑高楼、建立都市、印刷货币，然后自愿成为宇宙市场中的一块拼图。

**"Profnana":** Following the exact path of the golden Slumbernana, stubbornly holding onto the status quo, creating fatal vulnerabilities for themselves, and ultimately becoming useless.

> **「蕉授」：**就像金黄睡蕉一样，一成不变、一触即溃、最后一无是处。

**"Profnana":** Having undergone a flawed Bananenlightenment, the Doctor seeks to return these misguided worlds to their embryonic potential, giving them a fresh chance to develop. This is the atavistic experiment.

> **「蕉授」：**因为它接受了错误的*蕉育*，所以博士要让误入歧途的世界变回充满潜力的胚胎，给予它们再次成长的机会，这就是返祖实验。

**Rappa:** However, I have visited those planets.

> **乱破：**然而在下到访过那些星系。

**Rappa:** As I traveled from one planet to the other, a prevalent but unpalatable observation struck me: Not a single ninja could speak, they had all become fiendlings...

> **乱破：**在下一颗星一颗星地走过，却找不到任何能开口说话的忍者，全都是邪祟……

**Rappa:** But I know for a fact that they were not born this way.

> **乱破：**而且你又明明白白地知道，他们不是生来如此。

**Rappa:** The monkey that scurried past you was once a sailor. He knew how to ferment his own wine, and would do so regularly.

> **乱破：**刚从你脚边爬过去的猴子，几年前还是个水手，平日里总是自己酿酒喝。

**Rappa:** The one in the distance, fighting with other monkeys over a piece of fruit, used to be the smartest person on his planet, even having the knowledge to send people soaring into the sky.

> **乱破：**在远处为了个果子和其他猴子打成一片的，是那颗星球上最聪明的人，他曾经知道人要怎么飞到天上去。

**Rappa:** But from now on... they're monkeys. Just monkeys.

> **乱破：**但从今以后，他们就只是猴子。

**"Profnana":** Am I imagining things? Or did you learn how to speak like a normal person?

> **「蕉授」：**是我的错觉吗？同学，你好像学会正常的说话方式了。

**"Profnana":** Or... could it be that this familiar scene reminded you of your past?

> **「蕉授」：**还是说，此情此景——让你想起从前了？

**"Profnana":** Given the Doctor's brilliant mind, his ambitions are lofty and are sure to be realized. But the commoners? Hmph, all they do is lose themselves in unrealistic fantasies, becoming an impediment to a civilization's evolutionary progress.

> **「蕉授」：**博士是天才，他的理想足够崇高，也一定能实现。但平庸者只会沉沦于不切实际的幻想，成为文明进化的赘余。

**"Profnana":** Whether imbued with life or not, everything ought to be used to its full potential. Ah, how long has it been now? I'm surprised that lesson hasn't stuck...

> **「蕉授」：**人尽其才，物尽其用。这么久了，还没学会这个知识点吗……

**"Profnana":** Doctor's "failed creation"?

> **「蕉授」：**博士的「失败品」？

**Rappa:** ...

> **乱破：**……

**Dan Heng:** Your scheme ends here.

> **丹恒：**你的阴谋到此为止了。

**"Profnana":** Is that so? Ever considered why I allowed you to do as you please on campus? Even going as far as to guide you to me?

> **「蕉授」：**哦？知道我为什么会放任各位在校园里胡闹，甚至一步一步引导你们来这里吗？

**"Profnana":** Paperfold University is nothing more than an incubator, no great loss if discarded. But if it can eradicate the pests in the lab, then it'd be used to its full potential.

> **「蕉授」：**折纸大学就是个培养皿，丢掉也不可惜。但如果它能把实验室里的害虫一网打尽…那就是「物尽其用」。

**"Profnana":** Bana bana! That's all I have for you. Class dismissed.

> **「蕉授」：**蕉蕉蕉，时间到了，该下课了。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Mr. Reca:** Cut!

> **芮克先生：** CUT！

**Mr. Reca:** Brilliant, absolutely brilliant...

> **芮克先生：**精彩，真是太精彩了……

**Mr. Reca:** Such a brilliant film... It would be a shame for it to end now.

> **芮克先生：**如此精彩的影片…现在落幕就太可惜了。

**Mr. Reca:** Alright, cast, get ready to switch scenes!

> **芮克先生：**好了，演员们，准备换场吧！

*(Cutscene ends)*

**"Profnana":** Just what do you think you're doing, Memokeeper?

> **「蕉授」：**你在做什么，忆者？

**Mr. Reca:** I've already met all your demands, dearest producer. Please hold up your end of the deal as we've agreed: Lift this abominable curse that stifles my artistic expression.

> **芮克先生：**亲爱的制片人，您的要求已经全部完成，按照约定，请为我解除这毫无艺术细胞的诅咒吧。

**"Profnana":** Demands? I'm afraid not, Mr. Reca. Until you hand over those incorrigible and willful monkeys, you have not met all of my "demands".

> **「蕉授」：**要求？不不不，芮克先生，把那群难以*蕉化*的顽猴交出来。你才谈得上「完成了要求」。

**Mr. Reca:** But that's not our deal! I went to great lengths to help you infiltrate the school, disrupted The Family's surveillance, and even dealt with the Ranger who was causing you the most trouble. And now you want me to give up ownership of the actors?

> **芮克先生：**这不对啊。我费尽心力帮各位混入学校，妨碍家族监视，又解决了你们最头疼的游侠，现在你还要我交出演员的所有权？

**Mr. Reca:** We're talking exceptional talents that are incredibly rare to come by! They have so many more stories waiting to be unearthed! I can't allow their journey to end here.

> **芮克先生：**他们都是稀世罕见的好演员，还有许多留待挖掘的故事，我可不能让他们就这么杀青啊。

**"Profnana":** Mr. Reca, there is no room for negotiation here. I have no need to negotiate with a captive.

> **「蕉授」：**芮克先生，我没有在和你商量，我没必要和一位俘虏商量。

**"Profnana":** If you don't acquiesce, then in less than three system hours, you'll turn into a Slumbernana Monkey like those students. Hand them over now if your directing career even means anything to you.

> **「蕉授」：**不出三个系统时，你也会和这群学生一样变成睡蕉小猴。不想自己的导演生涯就此结束，就乖乖把他们交出来。

**Mr. Reca:** No, I refuse! Profnana, have you ever wondered why I ended up under your control?

> **芮克先生：** NG——我拒绝！蕉授，你知道我为何会落入你们之手吗？

**"Profnana":** Tracking down the Doctor's whereabouts shows you have some skill. However, believing you could follow us unnoticed was quite presumptuous, wasn't it?

> **「蕉授」：**能追查到博士的下落，算你有点本事。但偷偷摸摸跟过来还认为不会被发现，未免有些太过傲慢了？

**"Profnana":** Even if we were to need animals for our experiment, you're nowhere near qualified... "great director."

> **「蕉授」：**即便以实验动物的标准，你也远远不够资质啊，*大导演*。

**Mr. Reca:** What a pity. I put my life on the line just so I could make this wonderful film a reality. I decided a long time ago that I'd do anything for art, so your threat means nothing to me.

> **芮克先生：**可惜，我铤而走险，一心只为拍摄精彩的影片。从一开始，我就已经做好为艺术献身的准备了，所以你的威胁毫无意义。

**Mr. Reca:** Every film crew knows full well the risks they run the moment filming begins. And in turn, a dedicated director would never stop rolling the cameras, even if it meant having to insert an obelus next to a crew or cast member's name in the credits!

> **芮克先生：**对于剧组，开机就要承担风险，真正的导演哪怕让字幕里的名字加上示亡号，也绝不会让摄影机停下来。

**"Profnana":** In the end, you too are just a pitiable monkey, struggling for worthless dreams.

> **「蕉授」：**说到底，你也是一只为没用的梦想挣扎的可怜猴啊。

**"Profnana":** Be prepared to sacrifice yourself then.

> **「蕉授」：**那你就做好献身的准备吧。

**"Assistant Director":** ...

> **「副导演」：**……

Switching to Boothill's set...

Unbeknownst to everyone, the cosmic cowboy who became uncontactable is currently lurking in the shadows of the Dreamscape...

## 043　Dead Banana Society　死亡蕉社

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Dreamflux Reef　|　角色：Boothill; March 7th; Montana; "Profnana"　|　前置：Trailblaze Continuance ''A Clockwork Banana'' completed

### Scope out the lil' fudgeheads in the Slumbernana Association

> *Putting in some serious work, you crash the turf of Slumba... or whatever. Can't tell if these folks have been brainwashed by someone. They chat in a way even weirder than you do. Well, go have a chat with them. It might actually make you seem like the sensible one here.*

...

**???:** "Hatred is the frailty of the weak."

> **？？？：**「仇恨是一切弱者的弱点」。

**Boothill:** Fudgin' banana... That line again!?

> **波提欧：**他香蕉的…又来了？

**Slumbernana Association: Ape 11:** And that is everything that Slumbernana Monkey taught me...

> **睡蕉社•猿11：**这就是我从睡蕉小猴身上学到的一切……

**Slumbernana Association: Ape 11:** Think about this, everyone: the emotions we experience, be it joy, sadness, or an epiphany... every one of them is produced from a subtle shift in chemical composition within this vessel of ours.

> **睡蕉社•猿11：**仔细想想吧，诸位，我们所感受到的欢欣、悲伤、灵光一闪…它们近乎全部依赖于这副身体中，化学物质的微妙变化。

**Slumbernana Association: Ape 11:** With some manipulation, we should be able to easily create or erase emotions. That means triggering a sudden change in your own temperament isn't out of the question!

> **睡蕉社•猿11：**略施手段，它们就会产生、消逝，你甚至能让自己在短时间内性情大变。

**Slumbernana Association: Ape 11:** So here's my conclusion: Even if a behavior is generated after thorough deliberation, it is no more sophisticated than the simple act of "jumping."

> **睡蕉社•猿11：**所以，这就是我的结论了：任何深思熟虑的行为，并不比「跳起来」这个简单的动作高级多少。

**Boothill:** ...What a load of horse manure.

> **波提欧：**…什么屁话。

**Boothill:** I'm checkin' somewhere else.

> **波提欧：**我去别的地方看看。

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** Mm-hmm... Huh? Wait!

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**好哦…哎，等等？

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** Where are you going? It wasn't easy to get in, so don't do anything that'll draw unnecessary attention. At least... stop pointing your gun at people?

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**你要去哪，好不容易把你内推进来，别再做惹眼的事啦。至少…别再掏枪指着别人？

**Boothill:** Appreciate it, Nameless gal. You have quite the authority 'round here, eh? Has the Astral Express begun to take on monkeys?

> **波提欧：**多谢了，无名客姐们。没想到你在这话语权还挺大——星穹列车开拓到猴子身上了？

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** I wasn't expecting that either, but the Assistananas insist I'm a great talent. They even gave me a membership card with a high level of clearance...

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**我也不想那么有天赋嘛。但助蕉非说本姑娘是个优秀人才，一上来就给我发了级别很高的社员证……

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** ...Wait, talented for a club that advocates the abandonment of all thought!?

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**呃，在主张放弃思考的社团很有天赋？

**Boothill:** Ain't wrong, am I? You were so darn mesmerized by their speech back there.

> **波提欧：**也没说错吧？姐们，刚才你听得都走不动道了。

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** Well... I was just listening to what they had to say. It's not like they managed to convinced me anyway. Plus, if they don't talk in rhyme, it's probably just nonsense.

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**听听而已嘛，我又没信。他们说话都不押韵，肯定不靠谱。

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** So, what brought you here? You don't look like you're a fan of these monkeys.

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**所以…你是怎么找到这的？总不能是因为喜欢小猴吧。

**Boothill:** Why not? Galaxy Rangers are forkin' monkey fans. We go bananas at the mere sight of them.

> **波提欧：**是啊，他香蕉的，巡海游侠都这样，见到猴子就恨不得翻个跟头。

**Montana:** So... He's on the hunt for some kind of fugitive? That explains why he's dressed like a sheriff...

> **蒙塔娜：**所以…这位先生其实是在追查什么通缉犯吗？难怪穿得像个警长……

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** Ah, no, no. I guess... you could say he IS a fugitive?

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**啊，没有没有。其实…他自己就是通缉犯？

**Montana:** ...Wh—What?

> **蒙塔娜：**…啊？

**Boothill:** Yada yada, tell her my life story then. I'm gonna go check the place out.

> **波提欧：**你慢慢跟她解释吧。我先自己转一圈。

*(Talk to March 7th again, optional)*

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** Aww, there's nothing to be afraid of! He just looks a little scary, but Dan Heng says he's more bark than bite.

> **睡蕉社•能人244•三月七：**没事啦，他只是看上去凶巴巴的。听丹恒说，他动不动就喵喵叫呢。

*(Talk to Slumbernana Association: Ape 11, optional)*

**Slumbernana Association: Ape 11:** And that is everything that Slumbernana Monkey taught me...

*(Talk to the Enthused Slumbernana Association Member, optional)*

**Enthused Slumbernana Association Member:** Ah my friend... you really are bold.

**Enthused Slumbernana Association Member:** How'd you find out I got my hands on an ultra rare BanaCard? Look, it's even embossed in gold!

**Boothill:** Hey brother, you ain't right in the head.

> **波提欧：**哥们，有病是吧？

**Enthused Slumbernana Association Member:** You're right. Ever since I got it, I haven't been able to think of anything else...

> **激动的睡蕉社成员：**还真让你说着啦，自从有了它，我可是茶不思饭不想……

*(Talk to Slumbernana Monkey)*

**Slumbernana Monkey:** Bananana! Bananana!

> **睡蕉小猴：**蕉蕉蕉！蕉蕉蕉！

**Boothill:** Not a single thing around here makes sense — this is downright bananas.

> **波提欧：**半个奇怪的地方都没有——这可真香蕉的怪。

### Chill with March 7th

**Boothill:** Hey, Nameless gal. Can we have a little talk over yonder?

> **波提欧：**无名客姐们，借一步说话？

**Slumbernana Association: Denisovan 244, March 7th:** Just me? Why can't we just talk here...?

Darkness enveloped everything in silence. Even though no sound could be heard, it was easy to imagine what words were echoing at that moment—

"Even if you lose the ability to see everything, you won't be any less happy."

No matter how you look at it, the man was always one step behind. The trap he willingly walked into extinguished the very firepower he intended to use.

Thus, after finding his nemesis, his fate was clear — just like those who were once human, he too would transform into a monkey.

Chimpanzees that consumed the drug grew into a form similar to Homo sapiens within three hours, gained the intelligence equivalent to that of a 14-year-old Homo sapiens, and can make primates of lesser intellect conduct rather complicated operations through Synesthesia Beacons and electric shock collars. **Boothill:** What's do you mean, pardner? Am I going to have to start learning how to greet The Family now?

> **波提欧：**怎么了哥们，再过一会儿，我就得学着和家族打招呼了？

**Micah:** ...

> **米凯：**……

**Micah:** On the contrary, that's something that will never happen in Dreamflux Reef.

> **米凯：**恰恰相反。在流梦礁，那是绝不可能发生的。

**Micah:** At the end of the day, I'm just Dreamflux Reef's Night Watch... not its leader. I'm loath to interfere with people's right to choose.

> **米凯：**说到底，我只是一位守夜人，并非流梦礁的领袖，也不该干涉居民们选择的权利。

**Boothill:** Choose? Don't tell me you believe they chose to become monkeys?

> **波提欧：**选择？你难道觉得他们变成猴子是心甘情愿？

**Micah:** That's exactly my point. These are people who seek their true selves. I don't believe they would seek happiness by erasing their individuality.

> **米凯：**这正是我要说的，对于这些追求自我的人们，我不相信他们愿意通过抹除自我来获得幸福。

**Micah:** But I won't stop them from heading towards destruction... as long as they made the decision with a clear head.

> **米凯：**我不会制止他们奔向毁灭，但前提是…他们能够清醒地作出决断。

**Boothill:** Hah, that's my boy!

> **波提欧：**哈，就是这样！

**Micah:** If there are people who aspire to become monkeys, I won't deny them of that dream.

> **米凯：**如果有人想变成猴子，我不否认那是一种志向。

**Micah:** But I hope Miss Robin would at least give them that right to choose once again.

> **米凯：**但至少，知更鸟小姐能将选择的权利重新摆到他们面前。

**Robin:** There is indeed a way to resolve the impending crisis without drawing The Family's eyes to Dreamflux Reef.

> **知更鸟：**的确有一种办法，能让流梦礁在不被察觉的情况下，化解眼前的危机。

**Robin:** We know the tuning of Harmony can erase the static noise. If the people of Dreamflux Reef are willing to follow my lead in singing a Charmonic tune together, we may be able to erase the heinous noises reverberating around Dreamflux Reef.

> **知更鸟：**既然「同谐」的调律能够消除杂音。如果居民们愿意在我的引领下合唱谐乐，也许就能驱散回荡在流梦礁的恶曲。

**Robin:** But knowing how a number of residents here view The Family... It won't be easy for the plan to work.

> **知更鸟：**但考虑到部分居民对家族的成见，这未必是件容易的事。

**Micah:** More importantly, if The Family's tunes become the only song left on Dreamflux Reef... We would've survived the crisis, but Penacony's only land of freedom would also cease to exist.

> **米凯：**更重要的是，如果流梦礁也只剩下家族的谐乐一种声音…那即便我们渡过这场难关，匹诺康尼最后一处自由之地也名存实亡了。

**Boothill:** Bah, just have every sound and voice come together in a single song! Listen. I have a fudgin' brilliant idea!

> **波提欧：**既然如此，让所有声音一起响不就完了！听着，我现在有个绝妙的主意。

**Boothill:** Come with me, singing queen! Whether it works or not depends on how much of a deviant you are.

> **波提欧：**跟我来，大明星，接下来就看你够不够离经叛道了。

The two arrive before the open-air stage in Dreamflux Reef...

**Boothill:** You ready for it? Now this is the only way to get everyone in Dreamflux Reef to sing together...

> **波提欧：**瞧好了，大明星，现在，能在流梦礁举办大合唱的法子就这么一种……

**Robin:** This...

> **知更鸟：**这……

**Boothill:** Need I say any more?

> **波提欧：**不用我多说了吧？

**Boothill:** Don't tell me that you've been so used to your classy posh instruments that this thing is beneath you.

> **波提欧：**可别说你高高在上惯了，用不来眼前这玩意儿。

**Robin:** No, that's not it... I know a thing or two regarding everything related to tunes.

> **知更鸟：**这倒不会…凡是和音律相关的，我多少都会。

**Robin:** But... this really is absurd.

> **知更鸟：**但…这还真是胡闹啊。

**Robin:** ...

> **知更鸟：**……

**Robin:** Perhaps an absurd crisis calls for an equally absurd solution.

> **知更鸟：**但或许胡闹的危机，就应该用胡闹的方式来解决吧。

**Boothill:** That's the way. Leave the rest to me. I'll make sure to get everyone we need.

> **波提欧：**这就对了，需要的人我保准给你带齐。

**Boothill:** Some people saved their world by giving a dance, and some people defeated their enemies by crying... It's finally our turn now!

> **波提欧：**有人跳支舞就拯救了世界，有人哭一场就灭了对手，现在轮到我们了！

**Boothill:** Got a crisis? Throw a party, yee-haw!

> **波提欧：**危机存亡的时候，我们办一场他宝贝的派对！

### Round up people and throw a fudgin' party!

*(Talk to Robin again, optional)*

**Robin:** Perhaps an absurd crisis calls for an equally absurd solution.

**Boothill:** Howdy, partner! What's wrong, pals? Ooh, that's one distressed look you have there! Haven't been swiggin' enough, eh?

> **波提欧：**呦——哥几个，怎么颤颤巍巍的？今儿没喝够？

**Anxious Citizen:** Shut up, cowboy. Enough snide remarks. Come over and help us out.

> **焦急的居民：**那边的牛仔，有空说风凉话，不如过来搭把手。

**Boothill:** Oh? That's exactly what I'm here for! Here, head over to this place and just wait for it.

> **波提欧：**巧了，我就是来搭把手的。给你们指个地儿，去那儿待着吧。

> **波提欧：**我早说过，人犯不着太聪明，有时候去舞池里跳一曲，事儿同样能解决。

**Monella:** A cowboy? Oh, right... the same guy as the other time! Could Miss Robin be...?

> **莫奈拉：**牛仔？是那时候的…难道说知更鸟小姐……

**Monella:** Come with me, everyone! I think he might just be making some sense!

> **莫奈拉：**大家，跟我来吧，他…或许言之有理呢！

**Anxious Citizen:** Monella, why are you...

> **焦急的居民：**莫奈拉，你怎么又……

> **焦急的居民：**算了，疯了这么多，也不差你一个了，那就过去看看吧。

*(Talk to Anxious Citizen again, optional)*

> **莫奈拉：**竟然看到了知更鸟小姐，我就知道……

**Anxious Citizen:** What do you know? Now's not the time to be a fan. How's this place even going to be a shelter?

> **焦急的居民：**你知道什么了？这会儿就别追星了吧，我是看不出来这哪像个能避难的样子。

**Boothill:** Patience, pal. Wait till the crowd starts spilling in. That's when it'll happen.

*(Listen to Micah, optional)*

**Micah:** ...That's right. I'm not trying to persuade anyone, or offering a suggestion while standing on high ground.

**Micah:** I'm simply offering an option that has never crossed your minds. It's your choice if you want to give up on saving yourselves.

**Boothill:** He's getting the hang of it. Guess I'll just stay out of it then.

> **波提欧：**别着急，哥们，现在还不够热闹——你就瞧好吧。

*(Talk to Callous Citizen)*

> **波提欧：**哥们，别愣着了，那边有场大派对，相当爱你，不去看看？

**Callous Citizen:** Micah already told us about it. Chiming in on The Family's harmonic tunes? You best get the hell out of my face!

> **冷酷的居民：**米凯已经和我们说过了，参加家族的谐乐？滚远点儿。

**Boothill:** Sheesh, isn't that one of my lines? But sure, if you prefer it this way...

> **波提欧：**哈，还抢起我的词儿了？那也好办。

**Boothill:** I know I'm being a lil' demanding here, but get your hide parts over to that party right now and save your home, or I'll make sure to dedicate an elegy for every one of you!

> **波提欧：**我知道这事荒唐了点，但赶紧挪个地儿，要么去派对上把你们的地盘救回来，要么让派对多给你们奏个哀乐。

**Callous Citizen:** You...

> **冷酷的居民：**你……

**Boothill:** Never seen a thug before? I'm not Micah. Wanna try me and my gun?

> **波提欧：**没见过流氓？我不是米凯，想赌一赌我会不会开枪？

**Callous Citizen:** What's going on...? Are fellow humans more dangerous than those monkeys now?

> **冷酷的居民：**这到底怎么回事…没变成猴子的人还更疯了？

**Callous Citizen:** Fine, we'll go to the party. Just... keep that gun of yours away.

> **冷酷的居民：**就按你说的…先把枪拿开。

*(Talk to Callous Citizen again, optional)*

**Boothill:** Oh ho, you sure turned obedient here. So, how do you like this party, pal?

> **波提欧：**呦，倒是挺安分，喜欢这吗，哥们？

**Callous Citizen:** What? Are you here to watch over my shoulder now?

> **冷酷的居民：**怎么，你还要回来监视我不成？

**Boothill:** Pfft. That singing queen's gonna put up a once-in-a-lifetime performance. You bet I'm not missin' it.

> **波提欧：**我可不想错过开场，你最好也一样！

**Boothill:** I'll do anything to catch the opening. Make sure you don't miss it too!

> **波提欧：**挺顺利啊，算了，就不插一脚了。

### Hit up March 7th and Montana! Let's chill together!

*(Talk to Montana and March 7th)*

**Boothill:** My, my, my, look who's here!

> **波提欧：**呦，瞧瞧这是谁来了。

**Montana:** ...

> **蒙塔娜：**……

> **三月七：**啊…我找你好半天了。

> **波提欧：**你猜猜我信没信？

> **三月七：**那算了嘛——之前打的助蕉算我一半就行。

> **波提欧：**那一半也就「一半」。

> **三月七：**噫——我就说怎么一点变化都没有，走走走，咱们抓紧时间。

> **蒙塔娜：**……

> **蒙塔娜：**我…也会尽力帮忙的。

> **波提欧：**嚯，怎么搞定的？你把她的助蕉解决了？

**Montana:** March explained everything to me. I wasn't really influenced by the Assistananas... It was something else.

> **蒙塔娜：**三月同学都和我说了，其实我根本没受到助蕉影响啦…是有一些别的原因。

**March 7th:** Anyway, it's not your fault. Those bananas are incorrigible! Preaching about how having nothing is best... More like they wouldn't be able to control a single human if none of us thought that way.

> **三月七：**反正不是你的错。那帮家伙真是坏极了，说什么一无所有更好，实际上要是不把人变成那样，他们才没本事控制每一个人呢。

**Boothill:** Say whatever they like. They're nothing but smashed fudges once I put my bullets into them.

> **波提欧：**只要能被一枪爱死，有什么哲学都无所谓。

**Boothill:** Everything ironed out between you two now? Follow me then.

> **波提欧：**没事了就跟我来，带你们去个好地方。

### Tell Robin to be ready to kick off the party

> *This hunt is a blast, with tasty malt juice, hilarious monkeys, and a fudgin' awesome party! Yeah, it's a bit off the wall, and things don't always add up, but just like they say in the movie you watched last time... Dang, what was that line again...? "If everything can be explained, this world would lose its touch of enigma."*

**March 7th:** Wow... Everyone's here!

> **三月七：**喔…还真够热闹啊。

*(Talk to Robin and Micah)*

**Boothill:** Hah! What was it you said? Who in their right mind would sing in a choir in this era?

> **波提欧：**哈，哥们说什么来着？这年头哪还有人玩合唱？

**Boothill:** Not when you tell them there's a party. That's when people come flockin'.

> **波提欧：**但你要说有派对，他们肯定抢着来。

**March 7th:** What? A party?

> **三月七：**啥？派对？

**Robin:** You're back, Mr. Boothill.

> **知更鸟：**波提欧先生，你回来了？

**Robin:** We wouldn't have this turnout if it wasn't for Mr. Micah.

> **知更鸟：**其实是米凯先生帮了许多忙。

**Robin:** Most of them are unwilling to trust me, much less the power of Charmony. But... they chose to put their faith in Mr. Micah.

> **知更鸟：**他们中有很多人不信任我，更不信任谐乐的力量。但…他们愿意相信米凯先生。

**Micah:** I think it's less about believing me than them not wanting to give up on this place.

> **米凯：**恐怕不是相信我，而是他们从没想过放弃这里。

**Micah:** Everyone's got something they want to prove. It's the first time Dreamflux Reef has ever had a way to overcome difficulties on our own, without relying on The Family.

> **米凯：**大伙儿都憋着股劲呢，能用家族以外的方式破除万难，对于流梦礁这还是第一次。

**March 7th:** So, what exactly are you gonna do?

> **三月七：**所以你们到底是要干什么啊？

**Micah:** You're likely to find it... a little unbelievable. I still can't wrap my head around how I actually agreed to this. I must be crazy.

> **米凯：**你可能也会觉得…有些难以置信。我竟然会认同那种方法，真是疯了。

**Boothill:** What's so scary about that!? I have to do something crazy every once in a while. THAT keeps me from really going nuts.

> **波提欧：**怕什么！我每隔一段时间就得做件够疯狂的事，不然我就会疯了。

**Boothill:** Alright, let's get the party going! Once the hype is at its peak, I'm gonna treat everyone to a drink! Heh, I'm starting to like you guys now.

> **波提欧：**开始招呼吧！等到最高潮的时候，我非得请大家伙儿都喝一杯，你们这儿的人不赖。

**Micah:** Thank you in advance, Mr. Boothill, but I'd like to remind you on behalf of Jessie that your seven cups from before haven't been paid yet.

> **米凯：**提前向你致谢，波提欧先生，但我得替翠丝提醒您一下，之前那七杯还没买单。

**Micah:** But I'm willing to take care of that bill, of course.

> **米凯：**不过，我愿意一并代劳。

**Robin:** I've hosted several acting gigs in the past, but this is the most unusual of the lot. Will it really work?

> **知更鸟：**演艺活动我也举办过不少，这次可以说是最特别的那一档。真的能够奏效吗？

**Boothill:** Don't overthink it, singing queen. Let me tell you something funny. I'm sure you can tell I've been through some modifications on my body. When I just started out as a cyborg, my memory module fizzled out every so often. And I didn't even mess with it.

> **波提欧：**放心吧，大明星。给你讲个乐子，看见我这副身体没，刚改造完的时候，储存记忆的模块说坏就坏，明明你碰都没碰它。

**Boothill:** The doctor told me to note things down if they were important, and she'd look up some possible causes before coming to fix me up a few days later.

> **波提欧：**医生跟我说，那你就把重要的事先记在纸上吧，我过几天就帮你修好，顺便找找原因。

**Boothill:** Guess what? I muddle-fudgin' noted things down for several days straight! And that's when it hit me: I was remembering too much crap! That's why my memory module kept shutting down on me.

> **波提欧：**你猜怎么着？他宝贝的，我记了几天几夜！这下我知道记忆模块为什么总是坏了，它要承担的东西太多。

**Robin:** Is there a reason you're telling us this?

> **知更鸟：**可是，您为什么要提起这个？

**Boothill:** Hah! Yea, and it's simple.

> **波提欧：**哈——简单！

**Boothill:** I'm telling you that it's not yourself you have to trust, but them.

> **波提欧：**因为我要告诉你，你现在需要相信的不是你自己，而是他们。

**Boothill:** Everyone here has a perfectly functional memory module. They didn't develop such robust brains to forget everything and become monkeys!

> **波提欧：**站在这儿的每一个人，他们的记忆模块可没坏，身上长了这么个坚韧的脑子，不是为了把一切忘掉变成猴子！

**Boothill:** So, you in?

> **波提欧：**怎么说？

**Robin:** Yes, let's get this party started.

> **知更鸟：**嗯，让我们开场吧。

**Boothill:** What was it again? An absurd solution to an absurd crisis? I won't call it that.

> **波提欧：**哈——胡闹的危机该用胡闹的方式解决？我倒不这么觉得。

**Boothill:** It's only right for Dreamflux Reef's song to be untamed and free!

> **波提欧：**流梦礁的歌，就该自由自在！

**Boothill:** Go get the party started, singing queen—

> **波提欧：**开场吧，大明星——

**Boothill:** No, I should call you "DJ queen" instead!

> **波提欧：**不…「唱片骑师」。

### Find a sweet spot and grab a mug of malt juice. The show is about to begin

**Boothill:** Let's go, Nameless gal. Or you can go grab a seat by yourself.

> **波提欧：**走了，无名客姐们——或者你自己去找个位置。

*(Take a seat)*

**Mr. Reca:** Beautiful, excellent... Music can really bring a film to life...

> **芮克先生：**很好，很好…音律实在是一部好电影的点睛之笔。

**Boothill:** Holy forkeroni! Aren't you writing off your character?

> **波提欧：**他宝贝个腿的，你小子不是杀青了？

**Mr. Reca:** I am, that's why I am watching this scene as a director instead of an actor.

> **芮克先生：**没错。所以现在我并非演员，而是以「导演」的身份临场观摩。

**Mr. Reca:** I can't wait to hear for myself how this song of Harmony is going to turn out.

> **芮克先生：**我也非常好奇，那会是怎样的「同谐」之歌。

**Mr. Reca:** Sometimes I get hit by sudden hunches that a great film is in the making. More often than not, these tend to be just scenes from different groups of people that are then edited together.

> **芮克先生：**在某些时刻，我会打心底里产生一部好片即将诞生的预感。而它们中的大部分，都是由一幕幕群像素材衔接而成。

**Mr. Reca:** Most of the characters in this film aren't what you would call protagonists. You don't really get to see full character arcs on them, but they still have a light in their hearts that can save humanity's soul.

> **芮克先生：**这片子里的大部分人都称不上主角，他们很难展现通常意义上的完整弧光，却同样有着拯救人心的灵魂光亮。

**Mr. Reca:** What do these people really need when they have lost everything? Guidance of the wise? Or aid from the strong?

> **芮克先生：**而当失去一切的时候，这些人最需要的又是什么？智者的指引？强者的援助？

**Mr. Reca:** I'd say neither. All they need is a camera to capture them. That's when others would be able to see the most wonderful show in the entire universe.

> **芮克先生：**二者皆非。在我看来，他们需要的只是一个镜头。然后，坐在屏幕前的人就能看见这世上最精彩的戏码。

**Boothill:** Bah, don't make things sound so complicated, director pal! If you were to ask me...

> **波提欧：**哈！没那么复杂，导演哥们，要我说——

**Boothill:** Fork it, that's exactly what *life* is!

> **波提欧：**——他宝贝的，这就是「人生」！

**Mr. Reca:** Mmm... It's the prelude. Can you hear it?

> **芮克先生：**嗯…您听到那隐约传来的前奏了吗？

**Mr. Reca:** "Those lesser thirds so plaintive, sixths diminished, sigh on sigh."

> **芮克先生：**「小三度音如泣如诉，减六度音叹息不止。」

**Mr. Reca:** "Told them something? Those suspensions, those solutions—'Must we die?'"

> **芮克先生：**「悬留音发问：我们非死不可？」

**Mr. Reca:** "Those commiserating sevenths—'Life might last! we can but try!'"

> **芮克先生：**「而七度音安慰道：生命能够持续——姑且一试，或未可知。」

**Micah:** So, Miss Robin, is there anything else I can do?

> **米凯：**那么，知更鸟小姐，还需要我做些什么吗？

**Robin:** I'm alright now. You don't have to accompany me any further. The butterflies that I felt from before are all gone now.

> **知更鸟：**嗯，不必再陪着我了，方才的紧张，现在已经完全消失了。

**Micah:** Is that because you're starting to get into the mood for the party early?

> **米凯：**是派对的氛围提前感染了您吗？

**Robin:** No, that's not it. I just... realized something I've never noticed before.

> **知更鸟：**恐怕不是呢，只是…发现了一些以前忽略的东西。

**Micah:** Oh?

> **米凯：**哦？

**Robin:** Everyone expected my life to change after going through something huge like that.

> **知更鸟：**所有人都认为，在经历了那种剧变之后，我的生活理应产生变化。

> **嘉宝娜：**是我的名字——以后我不用艺名了，既然要当我的粉丝，就要记住我的名字哦。

**(Trailblazer):** (Nanabloom-chan... or I should say, Gabbana has gained some recognition...)

> **开拓者：**（芭蕉花酱…不，嘉宝娜也得到了大家的认可……）

*(Talk to Nanabloom-chan again, optional)*

**(Trailblazer):** (Nanabloom-chan... or I should say, Gabbana has gained some recognition...)

> **三月七：**快看，是姬子姐姐和杨叔！

### Talk to Welt and Himeko

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**March 7th:** Himeko, Mr. Yang, you're here as well!

> **三月七：**姬子姐姐，杨叔，你们也来了！

**Welt:** That's right. We heard the anniversary celebration had been delayed, so we wrapped up the conference early and came straight here to see if we could catch the fun.

> **瓦尔特：**是啊，听说校庆突然延期，我们就尽早结束了研讨会，也想来凑个热闹。

**Himeko:** How was it? Did you enjoy your time on campus?

> **姬子：**怎么样，这段时间的校园生活还愉快吗？

- ▸ It was amazing.

**Welt:** Glad to hear it. People say that academic years are some of the happiest of their lives. I'm sure you'll be leaving with fond memories.

> **瓦尔特：**那就好，人们常说学校里的日子是人生中最快乐的时光，你今后一定不会忘记的。

- ▸ It wasn't fun at all!

**Welt:** Haha, not an eager student, (Trailblazer)? Never mind — life itself is a journey of study, the universe a boundless ocean of learning.

> **瓦尔特：**呵呵，看来开拓者不怎么喜欢上学呢。没关系，人生本就是一场求学的旅程，宇宙本身就是一个巨大的学校。

- ▸ We ran into some trouble...

**Welt:** Is that so? Sounds like student life wasn't boring after all. Look on the bright side — sometimes the most memorable things are the accidents of our youth.

> **瓦尔特：**是吗？看来你们的学习生涯没那么悠闲啊。没关系，比起平凡的生活，意外才是青春中最难忘的事情。

**Himeko:** Look! Miss Robin is here...

> **姬子：**快看，知更鸟小姐来了——

**Robin:** Hello, students. I'm very happy to be hosting the Dreamlight Anniversary this year.

> **知更鸟：**各位同学们，你们好。很高兴能为大家主持这次的「彩梦校庆」。

**Excited Student:** Oh gosh, it really is Miss Robin! The opening ceremony this year is much better than last time!

> **兴奋的学生：**天呐，是真的知更鸟小姐！这次开幕式可比上次强多了！

**Confused Student:** Hold on, who was the speaker last time?

> **疑惑的学生：**等等，上次演讲的是谁来着？

**Excited Student:** Uhhhhh... I don't remember.

> **兴奋的学生：**呃…想不起来了。

**Robin:** A long time ago, I also spent some time studying at Paperfold University. So, I can be considered an alumnus of the school.

> **知更鸟：**很久以前，我也曾在折纸大学修习过一段时光，所以也算是大家的同学。

**Robin:** Paperfold University back then was just like how it is right now. There was a lack of freedom, exams were a source of headaches, we had plenty of homework, and our thesis defense had stringent requirements... Well, it can't be helped since this place bears the burden of training the future of Penacony.

> **知更鸟：**那时的折纸大学和现在一样，算不上多么自由自在，也有让人头疼的考试，各种各样的作业，要求严苛的答辩…没办法，谁叫这里承担着培养未来的重任呢？

**Robin:** The annual Dreamlight Anniversary is the opportunity for students to temporarily toss their troubles aside. However, the anniversary celebrations will come to an end, just like how... we'll eventually awaken from our dreams.

> **知更鸟：**只有在每年的彩梦校庆期间，同学们才能暂时抛去那些烦恼。但校庆终有一天会结束，就像梦…总有一天也会醒。

**Robin:** But there is a type of dream that we will never awaken from, and should never awaken from...

> **知更鸟：**但有一种梦不会醒来，也不应该醒来——

**Robin:** And that is the dreams woven by our highest ambitions.

> **知更鸟：**这个梦，就是我们的梦想。

**Robin:** Some students wish to create beautiful objects, while others desire to lead amazing lives. There are some who even wish to become a better person. These are all amazing dreams.

> **知更鸟：**有的同学想要创造美好的事物、有的同学想要拥有美好的生活、有的同学想要让自己成为美好的人——这些梦想都很棒。

**Robin:** In our lives, dreams act as beacons that guide us forward. Once we have lit up the beacon, we may choose to rest at any time. This does not signify that we have given up, for as long as the light of the beacon can be seen, we will have the courage to restart our journey.

> **知更鸟：**梦想是生命的航标。只要点亮了这个航标，我们随时都可以休息。但这并不意味着放弃，因为只要看到航标的光，我们就有勇气重新启程。

**Robin:** I hope that all of you will be able to find your dreams at Paperfold University and will dedicate your lives to realizing them.

> **知更鸟：**希望大家能在折纸大学找到自己的梦想，然后用一生来贯彻它。

**Robin:** It is because of your sweet dreams that you can create color. It is because of your ideals that you can embrace freedom.

> **知更鸟：**因为你们有美梦，才能创造多彩。你们有理想，才能拥抱自由。

**Robin:** Welcome to Dreamlight Anniversary! Color your dreams, free your ideals!

> **知更鸟：**欢迎来到彩梦校庆！美梦多彩，理想自由——！

**Fellow Students:** Color your dreams, free your ideals!

> **同学们：**美梦多彩，理想自由！

- ▸ Color your dreams, free your ideals!

### The Ninja Hero lingers in the shadows of the Dreamlands, heralding a new upcoming hunt...

> *While the war has ended, new adversaries will inevitably arise in realms devoid of justice. In a cosmos lacking laws, everyone must rely on their own abilities... Or forge their own way of the ninja.*

**Rappa:** Despite the similarities to the Evil Realm, there are no clues here...

> **乱破：**此处虽与邪恶国度相似，但也并无任何线索…

**Rappa:** ...Yo, Ninja Gama.

> **乱破：**…哟，虾蟇•忍者。

**Mr. Reca:** How I wish that in the next film, you can call me something better, my wonderful actor.

> **芮克先生：**真希望下一场戏，你能给我换个称呼，我的好演员。

**Mr. Reca:** Are you alone?

> **芮克先生：**只有你一个人吗？

**Rappa:** Each Ninja Hero has their own prey. Silvergun Shura only came to repay a favor, after which he returned to his own hunt.

> **乱破：**每位忍侠都有自己的「猎物」，银枪•修罗殿下此次只是人情相助，当下已去继续他的狩猎了。

**Rappa:** Is anything the matter?

> **乱破：**阁下有什么事吗？

**Mr. Reca:** Hehe, after helping me shoot such an exciting film, I'm here to hand you your remuneration.

> **芮克先生：**呵呵，你帮我拍摄了这么精彩的影片，我是来奉上片酬的。

**Rappa:** A reward won't be necessary. I'm only acting according to my way of the ninja. Riches are not what I desire.

> **乱破：**酬谢就不必了，在下行侠只为心中忍道，从不贪慕财物。

**Mr. Reca:** Don't say that. I'm sure you'll be very interested in my remuneration. After all, this remuneration is...

> **芮克先生：**别这么说，你一定会对这份片酬感兴趣的，因为这份片酬的内容是……

**Mr. Reca:** ...about where Dr. Primitive went.

> **芮克先生：**——关于原始博士的去向。
